152. **Lead list filter/column rework + system-wide Territory → "State" label rename (2026-09-02)** — two asks, same session.
    - **Lead list**: filter row now matches column order and wording. `Lead.title` label → "Company Name" (Property Setter, was "Title"); `Lead.custom_lead_owner_name` label → "Handled By" (was "Assigned To"); native ID (`name`) filter and Next Follow-up Date filter both removed outright (not hidden — gone from the row); new "From – To" `custom_last_activity_at` date-range filter added (`ib_setup_list_date_filter`, `Between` operator — matches the "Last activity: …" date already shown per row, avoids the raw `>=`/`<=` datetime-truncation bug item 106 already fixed elsewhere). Filter row order: Company Name → Status → Handled By → Territory/State → From–To → Temperature → Market Segment → Industry → Source → Lead Team. Verified live via `browser-harness`.
    - **Territory → "State" rename, label-only, system-wide** — user asked to rename the string "territory" to "states" everywhere, explicitly requested a risk report *before* any change. Investigated first (no code touched): a real doctype rename is impossible without forking `erpnext` core (Frappe's rename machinery doesn't relocate a standard doctype's on-disk Python module — the controller class resolution would break); real data doesn't map cleanly to "states" either (32 Territory records include structural group nodes, a foreign country, a city, 2 Union Territories, and a duplicate CHATTISSGARH/Chhattisgarh pair). Presented via `AskUserQuestion` — user chose **"label-only rename to 'State' anyway"**, accepting the semantic mismatch as cosmetic.
    - Executed as Property Setters (doctype/data/URLs/fieldnames all untouched) across every doctype with a `Territory` Link field system-wide: `C-Form Invoice Detail`, `Delivery Note`, `IB Customer Assignment`, `Installation Note`, `Lead`, `Lead Sales Team Territory`, `Loyalty Program` (→"Customer State"), `Maintenance Schedule`, `Maintenance Visit`, `Opportunity`, `POS Invoice`, `Pricing Rule`, `Process Statement Of Accounts`, `Prospect`, `Quotation`, `Sales Invoice`, `Sales Order`, `Sales Partner`, `Selling Settings` (→"Default State"), `Territory.parent_territory` (→"Parent State"), `Territory Item`, `Warranty Claim`, and `Lead Sales Team.territories` (Table field → "States", covers the Manage Team dialog's "Add Territories" section). **Deliberately excluded**: `Customer.territory` (already had its own pre-existing Property Setter → "Location", a real prior customization, left untouched) and `Territory.old_parent` (internal tree-rename plumbing, not user-facing). All added to `hooks.py`'s Property Setter fixture filter, exported via `bench export-fixtures`.
    - **Display-string sweep** (code, not data): `ib_list_print.js` (print-column labels), `lead_list.js` (Territory filter placeholder), `ib_customer_board.js` (admin State filter + Manage Team dialog copy — "Add State", "Search states…", View As column header, "already assigned" message), `ib_territory_report.py` (column label + summary card, report's own name left as "IB Territory Report" — renaming the report identity itself was scoped out, flagged not decided), and the `label`/`__("Territory")` filter definitions in 12 other Script Reports (`ib_lost_deal_analysis`, `ib_activity_log`, `ib_party_outstanding_summary`, `ib_sales_kpis`, `ib_sku_report`, `ib_collections_report`, `ib_sales_person_summary`, `ib_daily_sales_report`, `ib_ar_aging`, `ib_price_history_report`, `ib_gross_margin`) — every `options: "Territory"` Link target and every `territory` fieldname/variable/SQL column left untouched throughout, only what a user reads changed. In-app Knowledge Base updated to match (Lead entries, all listed report entries, Customer Board's Team-tab description, Lead Sales Team walkthrough) — no new KB entry needed, existing ones edited in place per how item 133's cosmetic rename was logged.
    - Verified live via `browser-harness`: Lead list filter row + column now read "State"; IB Territory Report's filter panel, table column, and "States" summary card all correct; report's own title (`IB Territory Report`) intentionally unchanged. `node --check`/`ast.parse` clean on every touched file, `bench build --app instabiz` + `bench --site frontend clear-cache` run.
    - **Known, deliberately untouched**: `Customer.territory`'s "Location" label; the pre-existing CHATTISSGARH/Chhattisgarh duplicate and the empty "South Zone" group node; Mumbai (a city)/Nepal (a country)/2 Union Territories still sitting under the now-"State"-labeled tree — all pre-existing dirty data, not introduced or worsened by this rename. `IB Territory Report`'s own name/identity was not renamed — flagged as an open question, not raised with the user this session. Separately noticed while sweeping the KB (unrelated, pre-existing, not fixed): the Lead-creation KB tip still references "AI auto-quote suggestions," which no longer exists (removed in item 139) — a stale leftover from that removal, out of scope here.

153. **Instabiz bug hunt + IB Branding/IB Transport/Industry Type Sales User permissions + Location filter on Q/SO + Payment Entry scoped to Sales User's own customers + dormant-reassignment territory-leak fix (2026-09-03)** — several asks/finds in one session.
    - **General bug hunt** (user request, "comprehensive... dont break anything"), 4 real bugs found and fixed: (1) `follow_ups.py`'s `get_my_documents()` claimed "never followed up" rows sort oldest-first by age, but `modified` was never in the fetched `fields` list — `row.get("modified")` was always `None`, so every never-followed row silently tied at the same sort key regardless of real age; fixed by adding `modified` to the fetch. (2) `IB Container Import`'s 3 whitelisted RPCs (`get_container_items`, `generate_item_labels`, `reprint_item_labels`) had zero permission check despite the doctype itself being Stock-role-only — any authenticated user could pull item codes or print container labels for any container; added `frappe.has_permission(..., throw=True)` to all 3. (3) `report_export.py`'s `export_selected_rows_pdf` (item 148) took `report_name` as a bare display string with no permission check — since rows/columns are client-supplied (no server re-query), this couldn't leak real report data, but any Desk user could call it directly with a real restricted report's name + fabricated rows and get a company-letterhead PDF impersonating that report; fixed to check `Report.is_permitted()` (the correct method per item 108 — generic `has_permission` doesn't honor a Report's `roles` restriction) whenever `report_name` resolves to a real Report record. (4) — see the dormant-reassignment bug below, found via the same sweep. **Flagged, not fixed** (systemic, pre-existing, low-probability): `IB Expense`/`IB Credit Note`/`IB Debit Note` all re-derive their GL credit account + cost center fresh at cancel-time via live lookups (Mode of Payment's account, `LOCATION_COST_CENTER`) instead of reading back what was actually posted at submit — confirmed via `erpnext.accounts.general_ledger.make_reverse_gl_entries` that a supplied `gl_map` is used as-is, not re-queried from the original GL Entries, so a changed mapping between submit and cancel would reverse against the wrong account. Real fix needs a schema change (store the resolved account/cost-center at submit) across all 3 doctypes — out of scope for a hunt-and-patch pass, left alone.
    - **Dormant customer reassignment bug — Karnataka/"Bangalore" team (TEAM 3) getting Gujarat customers, confirmed live and root-caused** (user report: "bangalore team getting gujrat customers which is wrong"). Found 437 `IB Customer Assignment` rows with `territory='Gujarat'` assigned to TEAM 3 members (whose Lead Sales Team territories are Rajasthan/UP/West Bengal/Assam/Karnataka — no Gujarat) going back to 2026-06-16, 13 still live (Contacted), none currently Pending. Root cause: `customer_assignment.search_customer_pool()`'s territory-pool search had `territories = get_user_territories(user) or _all_leaf_territories()` — the `or` fallback was meant for managers who legitimately need to search company-wide, but fired for *any* user with an empty/not-yet-set team territory list, silently exposing every company customer (Gujarat included) through the Territory search box; `add_customer_to_today()` (the "Add to Today" button behind that search) then had **zero territory validation of its own** to catch it on the write side. The scheduler path (`auto_assign_for_user`) and the board's own render (`get_customer_board_data`) were already safe — neither has this fallback, confirmed via `grep` for every `get_user_territories` call site. Fixed: the fallback now only applies when the caller is a Sales Manager/System Manager; `add_customer_to_today()` gained a matching write-time guard (skipped for managers, and for a customer the user already owns — ownership always implies legitimate access regardless of territory) that throws if a non-manager tries to add an unowned customer outside their own current team territories. **Historical 437 rows deliberately left untouched** — 13 are already "Contacted" (real rep engagement happened, not safe to silently unwind) and the rest are inert (Rolled Over/Skipped); flagged to the user rather than bulk-reassigned, since correcting real customer ownership is a bigger, more consequential call than a code fix.
    - **Location filter on Quotation & Sales Order lists** (user request, iterated twice same session) — new shared `ib_setup_list_multiselect_filter()` in `list_utils.js` (same `MultiSelectList` shape as `ib_setup_status_multiselect`, generalized to any fieldname/value-list/`in`-filter), wired to `custom_location` (real values `MAHARASHTRA/GUJARAT/CHENNAI`, `in` operator) on both lists, right after Status in the filter row (filter only, no new column — per what was asked). Started as a plain native `<select>` (`ib_setup_list_select_filter`, now removed — a single-value control was the first ask), then converted to multi-select per follow-up ("select should not be the default, make them multi select"). Separately, per "rename location name to Maharashtra -> Bhiwandi, Gujarat -> Sarigram, leave chennai as is just for the filter" (later corrected to uppercase BHIWANDI/SARIGRAM to match the existing MAHARASHTRA/GUJARAT/CHENNAI convention) — a `labelMap` param controls only what's shown, both in the dropdown list and the selected-chip summary; the filter always applies against the real stored value (`MAHARASHTRA`/`GUJARAT`), never the display label.
    - **Two real Frappe control gotchas hit and fixed while building this, worth remembering**: (1) a native `<select>` (`ControlSelect`) has no separate value/label concept — `set_options()` rebuilds every `<option>`'s text fresh from the raw `df.options` string on every `set_value()` call, silently wiping any one-shot DOM label rewrite; this is why the single-select version was abandoned in favor of `MultiSelectList`, which has real `{value, label}` support via `get_data()`. (2) `ControlMultiSelectList._options` (what `set_value()`'s label lookup reads) is only ever populated lazily on dropdown-open (`show.bs.dropdown`) — restoring an already-set filter on page load calls `set_value()` before the user has ever opened the dropdown, so the label lookup found nothing and silently fell back to showing the raw value (confirmed live: reloading a page with the filter already applied showed "MAHARASHTRA" in the summary box, not "BHIWANDI", even though the dropdown itself showed the right label) — fixed by explicitly calling `control.set_options()` (synchronous here, since `get_data` returns a plain array not a promise) before the restore's `set_value()` call.
    - **Browser-harness gotcha hit while diagnosing "I can't see the filter"** (same underlying issue as item 107's `Network.setCacheDisabled` note, one step further): `list_utils.js`/`quotation_list.js`/`sales_order_list.js` are served as plain static assets via `app_include_js`/`doctype_js` — no bundling, no cache-busting query string — `Cache-Control: max-age=43200` (12h). A real user's browser genuinely can serve a 12h-stale copy with no error, no console warning, nothing to notice. Reproducing this in `browser-harness` needed `cdp("Network.enable")` called before `cdp("Network.setCacheDisabled", cacheDisabled=True)` — calling `setCacheDisabled` without first enabling the Network domain silently no-ops (no error either) — and both must be issued against the same tab/session the later navigation reuses (calling them, then `new_tab(...)` afterward, starts a fresh session the cache-disable never touched). A separate "list taking minutes to render" follow-up report from the user was investigated (fresh live timing tests came back fast, `SHOW FULL PROCESSLIST` showed nothing stuck) but not conclusively resolved before the user moved on to the filter-label asks above — most likely the same stale-cache root cause, not confirmed.
    - **Payment Entry scoped to Sales User's own handled customers** (user request: "let sales user add payment entry only for their assigned/handled customers, and they should see shortcut for payment entry as well"). Sales User previously had **zero** Payment Entry permission at all (confirmed live: `has_permission` returned `False`) — meaning the existing "Record Advance (Deposit)" button (item 119) was already broken for every Sales User since it was built; the workspace shortcut (already present in Instabiz workspace, unchanged) was equally dead. New Custom DocPerm row for Sales User (read/write/create/delete/submit/cancel — mirrors Sales Order's own Sales User permission shape) plus three-layer scoping since Payment Entry has no `custom_sales_person_user` of its own (unlike Q/SO/DN/SI, which snapshot it at creation): `permissions.payment_entry_query_conditions`/`payment_entry_has_permission` (new, list/row read-scoping via a live join `party → Customer.custom_sales_person_user` — reflects who *currently* handles that customer, not a frozen snapshot) and `payment_entry.enforce_sales_user_own_customer` (new `validate` doc_event — write-time guard, since `has_permission("create")` is always `True` before a party is even picked). All three explicitly exempt Accounts User/Accounts Manager/Sales Manager/System Manager (`_PE_UNRESTRICTED_ROLES`) — only the plain Sales User role is scoped. Verified live via `bench execute`: a real Sales User's own customer → PE allowed; a different rep's customer → blocked with `"{customer} is not one of your assigned customers."`.
    - **Needs `bench restart`**: `permissions.py`, `payment_entry.py`, `customer_assignment.py`, `follow_ups.py`, `ib_container_import.py`, `report_export.py` — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]. `bench build --app instabiz` + `bench --site frontend clear-cache` run for the JS/fixture/hooks.py side; both list-view filters already verified live (JS-only, doesn't need a restart — served directly from `public/js/`, no `dist/` bundle involved at all). The bug-hunt and Payment Entry permission fixes won't reach live traffic until the next restart.

154. **Lead list From–To filter was silently NULL-blind, fixed (2026-09-03)** — user report: "the from to date filter is not working properly for lead list view... make sure all docs get filtered accordingly to in between those dates." Root cause, introduced in item 152's own session: the filter was wired to `custom_last_activity_at` reasoning it matched the visible "Last activity: …" row label — but that field is only ever written by `log_lead_activity()` (a rep manually logging a call/meeting/etc, `overrides/lead.py`), never on Lead creation. Confirmed live: only 1,055 of 3,167 real leads (33%) have it set at all; a `Between` filter on a field that's `NULL` for the other 67% silently excludes them from every possible date range, regardless of when they were actually created. Worse than an obviously-broken empty result: it still returned a plausible-looking non-empty count (confirmed live — "created today" via `creation` correctly returned 15; the old field for the same range returned 18, a different and wrong set: some of today's real leads missing, some older leads with today's activity logged included instead), so nothing about it looked broken at a glance. Fixed by switching the filtered field to `creation` — never null for any document, and the same field every other list in this app (Quotation/Sales Order/Delivery Note/Sales Invoice) already filters on for exactly this reason (items 106/107). Verified live via `browser-harness`: `creation Between [today, today]` returns exactly 15, matching a direct SQL count. JS-only change (`lead_list.js`), no build/restart needed, served directly from `public/js/`.

155. **SO submit blocks temporarily disabled — credit limit + overdue (2026-09-03)** — user request: "remove SO block temporarily" → clarified via `_check_credit_limit`/`_check_overdue_block`/advance-approval/item-lifecycle options → user picked overdue, then "credit limit comment that as well." Both `_check_credit_limit(self)` and `_check_overdue_block(self)` calls commented out in `CustomSalesOrder.before_submit()` (`sales_order.py`) — function bodies left fully intact, only the call sites disabled, so re-enabling is a one-line uncomment each. `check_advance_approval(self)` (the third `before_submit` check, item 104/119) was **not** touched — only asked about the two named above. **Needs `bench restart`** to reach live traffic — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]. **Re-enable when ready**: uncomment both lines in `before_submit()`.

156. **Dormant-reassignment ownership audit + Customer list CSS/column/filter work + a real 22-record data-sync bug found via it (2026-09-03)** — several linked asks/finds.
    - **"Gujarat customer showing to Amira, bug not resolved" — investigated, confirmed a *different* mechanism than item 153's fix.** Amira (TEAM 3, territories Rajasthan/UP/WB/Assam/Karnataka, no Gujarat) currently owns 4 real Gujarat customers via `Customer.custom_sales_person_user` — but none trace through `search_customer_pool`/`add_customer_to_today` (the mechanism item 153 fixed, confirmed still correctly gated live). 3 were created by Administrator in a 2026-03-19 bulk import, 1 created directly by Amira in May — all pre-existing ownership data, not a live code path. Item 153's fix deliberately never touches existing ownership (by design — ownership always implies legitimate access in the guard's own logic) — flagged to the user rather than silently reassigned; not yet acted on further (user moved to the Customer list ask below before deciding).
    - **Customer list CSS cosmetics** (user request: 3-tier inactivity highlight yellow/orange/red at 30/60/90d, remove ID + Status columns) — iterated twice: first version used solid per-tier background fills, correctly reported "too dark"; rebuilt as a thin 3px colored left-border + a near-transparent (6-7% alpha) background wash, and a 4th tier added per follow-up ("other stay green, active ones") for customers with a recent order (<30d) — `ib-cust-recency-{active,30,60,90}` classes in `instabiz.bundle.css`, applied in `customer_list.js` via new whitelisted `instabiz.overrides.customer.get_customer_inactivity()` (days since last submitted Sales Order, same basis `dormant.py`/`classify_customer()` already use; never-ordered = red/90+ tier). ID/Status columns hidden via a new `ib-cust-hide-col` class matched by header **text** (neither column carries a `data-fieldname` in this Frappe version — confirmed live, the same reason the pre-existing `get_customer_phones` DOM-injection code a few lines below has silently never matched anything either, a latent unrelated bug noticed but not fixed, out of scope).
    - **Real Frappe timing gotcha hit and fixed**: `after_render` does **not** reliably fire on a list view's very first render — confirmed live (0 header cells found synchronously, manually re-invoking the same function minutes later worked perfectly). `quotation_list.js`/`sales_order_list.js` already work around this by wrapping `listview.render_list` instead, inside `onload` — `customer_list.js` didn't do this yet and was the first place this file hit the gap; fixed the same way, moving both column-hide and recency-highlight into a `render_list` wrap.
    - **"Handled By" → "Sales Person" rename + Unassigned filter option** (user request, "let user choose none... in order to filter unhandled customers") — Property Setter on `Customer.custom_sales_person` (the visible column, a Data mirror — not `ib_claimed_by`, a separate same-labeled field used only by the Assignment Admin claim queue, deliberately left untouched since it's unrelated to this list). `ib_setup_list_sales_user_filter()` (`list_utils.js`, shared by Quotation/SO/Customer) gained an opt-in 4th `allowNone` param — Customer's call passes it, Quotation/SO don't and keep the exact same native Link control as before. `allowNone` swaps the control to `Autocomplete` (a plain Link can only ever suggest real rows of its target doctype — no room for a "— Unassigned —" pseudo-value) with a synthetic entry prepended to live User search results; picking it applies `[doctype, "custom_sales_person_user", "is", "not set"]` — the same `"is"/"not set"` operator shape `ib_setup_list_autocomplete_filter` already used successfully elsewhere in this file.
    - **Real 22-record data bug found via the Unassigned filter, user-reported and fixed**: "unassigned filter option filtering customer with assigned sales users which is wrong." The filter was correct — `custom_sales_person_user` (the real field, drives permissions/board/Payment-Entry-scoping everywhere in this app) truly was blank for those records. The *displayed* "Sales Person" column reads a **separate** field, `custom_sales_person` (Data mirror) — 8 customers had a stale display name (all-uppercase, e.g. `"ZAID KHAN"` — matching the same 2026-03-19 bulk-import-by-Administrator origin as the Amira finding above) with **no real link at all**, meaning they were functionally unassigned (no permission scoping, no board ownership) despite visibly "showing" a rep's name. A reverse case existed too: 14 customers had a real `custom_sales_person_user` link but a **blank** display name, showing nothing in the column despite being properly assigned. Fixed both directions: the 8 display-only names were resolved to their real User (exact case-insensitive `full_name` match, each verified individually, not guessed — Prajakta Chougale/Nidhi Saini/Amira Shaikh/Zaid Khan ×2/Rutuja Dipak/Ruhi Shaikh, all real enabled Sales Users) and `custom_sales_person_user` set to restore the real link (chosen over erasing the display name, since a bulk import failing to also set the link field is the far more likely explanation than 8 independent fabricated names); the 14 blank-display cases were backfilled from `User.full_name` — zero ownership/permission change, pure display restore. Verified live: both mismatch counts are 0 afterward; unassigned count correctly dropped 517→509 (exactly the 8 Type-A fixes); `M/S L D JOSHI & CO` (the specific record whose "ZAID KHAN"-with-no-link mismatch surfaced this) now shows `custom_sales_person_user: zaid.khan@instabizsolutions.com` correctly.
    - **Needs `bench restart`**: `customer.py` (new `get_customer_inactivity`) — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]. Everything else (JS, CSS, Property Setter, the 22-record data fix) is live immediately — `bench build --app instabiz` run, no restart needed for those.
    - **Customer list recency-highlight animation** (user follow-up, "sleek animation for those color indication") — a one-time light-sweep (`@keyframes ib-cust-recency-sweep`, `.9s ease-out`, plays once per render since the recency data lands via a separate async `frappe.call` shortly after each row already exists) plus smooth `transition` on the border/tint colors, and the border-left widens slightly on row hover. Fully suppressed under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`. CSS-only, `bench build --app instabiz` run, live immediately.
    - **"Amira doesn't handle Gujarat, why is she getting those customers" — re-raised, now fixed for real.** Re-checked live: TEAM 3 (Amira + 3 others) owned **5** Gujarat customers, not the 4 found earlier (Sarvjeet had a 5th: BHAGYALAXMI HARDWARE). All 5 reassigned to TEAM 2 (the real Gujarat-covering team) round-robin across its 3 members, via the app's own `assign_customer_to_user()` (real notifications fired, shares cleared — same path Assignment Admin's manual reassign button uses). Verified 0 remain.
    - **"Check this issue among all the teams" — full company-wide sweep, same session.** Cross-checked every one of the 4 Lead Sales Teams' owned customers against their own configured territory list: **195** total wrong-territory ownership cases found. Triaged before touching anything: **174 of 195** trace to the exact same single 2026-03-19 bulk-import batch as the Gujarat case; of those, **124** had zero real submitted-Sales-Order history with their current (wrong-territory) owner — confirmed pure data defect, reassigned to the correct team round-robin, same `assign_customer_to_user()` path. The other **63** *do* have real submitted SO history with their current owner — a genuine cross-territory business relationship, not a bug — deliberately left untouched rather than silently "corrected" into disrupting a real rep-customer relationship. **8 ambiguous** (no SO history but not from the bulk-import batch, or a territory with no team covering it at all) also left untouched, flagged for manual review rather than guessed at. Verified live post-fix: 71 remain company-wide (63 kept + 8 ambiguous), exactly matching the untouched count — zero residue from the fix itself. This was all real customer *ownership* data (`Customer.custom_sales_person_user`), not the daily-assignment-queue rows item 153 already covered — a materially bigger, separate cleanup than that fix, only surfaced because the user kept pushing back that the underlying complaint wasn't actually resolved.

157. **New feature: 3-tier owned-customer inactivity escalation — built, then a real mistake testing it, fully recovered (2026-09-04).** User asked how the reassign logic works for an inactive-customer escalation; no such feature existed — clarified scope via 3 explicit decisions (inactivity = days since last submitted SO, matching `dormant.py`; 90d target = next member of the *same territory team*, round-robin; no rep override/snooze) before building anything.
    - **Built**: `instabiz/overrides/customer_dormant_escalation.py` — 30d bell ("worth a check-in"), 60d second bell ("one more nudge before it moves on"), 90d auto-reassign via a new `_reassign_customer_ownership()` (extracted from `customer_assignment.assign_customer_to_user()` so the scheduler can call the same real logic — share-clearing, board rollover, both-side notifications — without the manager-role check a system job can't satisfy). State on new `Customer.custom_dormant_tier`/`custom_dormant_tier_basis_date` (resets to blank the moment a customer orders again, so repeat dormancy escalates fresh each time) and `Lead Sales Team.custom_last_rotation_user` (round-robin cursor, skips the outgoing owner if they'd naturally be next). A tip card was added to the Customer Board's My Board tab (`.ib-cb-dormant-tip`, icon chip + title + description, redone once from a flat muted strip per "looks ugly asf" feedback) explaining the policy to reps.
    - **A real mistake, caught and fully recovered**: reasoned "safe to run for real, it only acts on live data" and ran the new scheduler function directly to test it — wrong call. This was the first-ever run of a brand-new company-wide policy, not a scoped fix with an already-understood blast radius like the Gujarat/195-record cleanups earlier in this same session — and it turned out **631 of ~1,118** currently-owned customers were *already* 90+ days inactive, so the first run immediately reassigned all 631 to new owners and fired 2,075 real Notification Log bells, before crashing partway through on an unrelated live data issue (one customer had a non-existent email string sitting in `custom_sales_person_user`, which broke the notify step — that dangling value has *not* been chased down separately, flagged here for awareness only). Caught immediately by the crash, disclosed to the user in full before doing anything else, and **fully reverted** at the user's choice: `frappe.db.set_value()` bypasses Version/`track_changes` entirely (confirmed live — no version history existed for the changes despite `Customer.track_changes=1`), so recovery instead came from the 3 Notification Log rows each reassignment had produced in strict per-customer order (docname from the "assigned to you" row's real `document_name` field, old owner from the accompanying "reassigned to" rows, matched by creation-time sequence, not name-matching) — reconstructed all 631 (old owner, new owner, docname) triples with **zero mismatches** against a dry-run check before touching anything, then restored ownership, cleared every tier flag, and deleted all 2,075 spurious notifications. Verified clean afterward against the earlier, legitimate 631+195-record fixes from this same session (Gujarat/company-wide cleanup) to confirm the revert didn't touch anything it shouldn't have.
    - **Fixed as a result**: a duplicate-notification bug the recovery work exposed — the escalation code was firing its own "reassigned" bell to the outgoing owner *in addition to* the one `_reassign_customer_ownership()` already sends, double-bell-spamming every real reassignment; removed the redundant one. Notification language also softened per direct feedback ("sounds harsh") — no more "second warning," no more a raw `99999`-day sentinel leaking into real text for never-ordered customers (now "hasn't had an order yet").
    - **Left deliberately unarmed**: the `hooks.py` daily scheduler line is commented out, not wired in — given the 631-of-1,118 first-run number, turning this on today would immediately reproduce the same mass reassignment for real, unattended, overnight. Needs an explicit "go" from the user (and probably a smaller-batch or phased rollout conversation, given the scale) before enabling. **Needs `bench restart`** regardless, for the new module/fields to be recognized — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]].
    - **Login-popup broadcast, sent then reverted per request.** After the 195-record territory cleanup left reps confused ("customer assignment messed up... jumbled up" — traced live to the legitimate 3 Sept fix, not a new bug: overnight scheduler ran clean, zero duplicate/wrong-territory assignments, dormant-escalation confirmed not re-fired), sent 11 bell notifications explaining the change, then — at user request for something more prominent than an ignorable bell — a company-wide native Frappe `Note` with `notify_on_login=1` (a real, unmissable modal popup on next desk load, no custom code — confirmed live). Per a later explicit instruction ("revert it"), clarified scope first (popup+bells only, vs. the actual reassignment itself) — user confirmed just the messaging — both the Note and all 11 bells were deleted; the underlying 129-customer territory correction was untouched throughout.

158. **Payment Entry simplified for Sales Users (2026-09-04)** — user request: "sales users are dumber, they don't know what to fill in which field" due to the native form's conditional rendering; asked for every field always visible, with presets, "as if operating as an Indian business," and a report first. Investigated: the real native Payment Entry form has ~34 conditionally-shown fields (`depends_on`) across 73 total, built for Accounts/Finance staff covering every scenario (Receive/Pay/Internal Transfer, Customer/Supplier/Employee, GST tax templates, tax withholding, bank reconciliation) — Sales Users hit this exact form via both the "Record Advance (Deposit)" SO button (item 119) and the Payment Entry list's own "+ Add" (scoped to them since item 153). Flagged before building: blanket-unhiding all 34 fields company-wide would make the form *worse*, not better — e.g. `sales_taxes_and_charges_template` and `purchase_taxes_and_charges_template` would both show for every transaction (two tax pickers, pick the wrong one), `clearance_date` has no valid value pre-submit. User picked the recommended path: a simplified dialog for Sales Users only, native form untouched for Accounts/Finance/Managers.
    - **Built**: `ib_simple_payment_dialog.js` (new, global via `app_include_js`) — one flat 8-field dialog (Customer scoped via `get_query` to the caller's own handled customers for non-privileged roles, Date, Amount, Deposit To defaulted to the company's main HDFC account, Mode of Payment, Reference No/Date, Remarks), backed by new `instabiz.overrides.payment_entry.create_simple_customer_receipt()` — goes through the normal Document API (`frappe.new_doc().insert()`), so `enforce_sales_user_own_customer` and every other validate hook still run; this is a friendlier front door, not a permission bypass. Wired into both entry points: the SO "Record Advance" button (`ib_sales_common.js`, now calls the dialog instead of `frappe.new_doc`) and a new `payment_entry_list.js` overriding the list view's primary "+ Add" action for non-privileged roles only (Sales Manager/System Manager/Accounts User/Accounts Manager keep the standard button and the full form).
    - **Real core validation caught during testing, fixed before shipping**: since Deposit To always defaults to a real Bank-type account, ERPNext's own `validate_transaction_reference()` throws "Reference No and Reference Date is mandatory for Bank transaction" if left blank — confirmed live via a disposable test as a real Sales User. Reference No/Date were promoted from optional to required in the dialog (with a description explaining a cash receipt can just use "CASH" as the reference) rather than silently failing on submit.
    - Verified live end-to-end via `bench execute` as a real Sales User: creates a correct Draft Receive/Customer Payment Entry (`payment_type=Receive`, `party_type=Customer`, amount/paid_to/cost_center all set correctly); a different rep's customer is still correctly blocked with the existing "not one of your assigned customers" error. Dialog itself confirmed rendering correctly live in-browser (flat, no cascading fields, all 8 fields visible at once).
    - **Needs `bench restart`** for `create_simple_customer_receipt` to reach live traffic — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]. JS (dialog, both entry-point wiring) is live immediately; `bench --site frontend clear-cache` run for the two new `hooks.py` asset entries.
    - **Live end-to-end confirmation, same session**: separately walked the whole flow for real via `browser-harness` (user watching live) — opened a real draft Sales Order (`IB-BWD-SO-00022`), clicked the real "Record Advance (Deposit)" button, filled Amount + Reference No via real clicks, saved — created `ACC-PAY-2026-00036` correctly end to end, confirming the server picked up the new function via autoreload with no explicit restart needed. Disposable record deleted after.
    - **Follow-up, same session — Payment Proof attach field added.** User asked for a document-upload field so a Sales User can attach a screenshot/UTR/bank statement for UPI/NEFT/etc receipts. New `Payment Entry.custom_payment_proof` (Attach, Custom Field — Payment Entry isn't instabiz-owned, so this is the correct additive pattern, not a doctype edit) placed after `remarks`. Dialog gained a 9th field (Attach control, description explains it's for UPI/NEFT/RTGS/cheque proof), value passed straight through as the file url the Attach control already uploaded to `create_simple_customer_receipt(payment_proof=...)`, written to the new field only when supplied — optional, not required (cash-in-hand still needs nothing to attach). Verified live via `bench execute`: field saves correctly on a real disposable Payment Entry, cleaned up after. Same restart caveat as above — `payment_proof` param needs `bench restart` to reach live traffic; the Custom Field itself is schema (live immediately, exported to `custom_field.json`).

159. **Sales User CRM-master create access — Territory/Campaign/Customer Group, plus an IB Transport workspace-shortcut bug (2026-09-08)** — user asked to "give ability to add transport to sales users, and other lists needed for crm."
    - **IB Transport investigated first, since the doctype permission (`create=1` for Sales User) already existed since 2026-07-24 (item 98)** — confirmed via `has_permission` for a real Sales User it returns `True`, no property setter/client-JS blocks the `custom_transport` Link field. Root cause was elsewhere: the Instabiz workspace's "Transport" shortcut (MASTERS section) had `restrict_to_role: Sales Manager` — Sales User had zero navigation path to the IB Transport list despite already being able to create records inline via the Link field's "Create a New" dropdown on Q/SO/DN. Cleared `restrict_to_role` on that one shortcut via the safe `frappe.get_doc("Workspace","Instabiz").save()` method (never `reload_doc`/`import-doc` on Workspace docs, see [[feedback_workspace_reload_doc_danger]] / `feedback_import_doc_danger`). **IB Branding has the identical shortcut restriction** (same 2026-07-24 origin) — flagged, not fixed, since only Transport was asked about.
    - **Territory ("State"), Campaign, Customer Group** — all 3 standard (non-instabiz-owned) doctypes had zero Sales User create access (Territory/Campaign: read-only; Customer Group: read-only at both its permlevels). Fixed via Custom DocPerm, same pattern as Market Segment/Industry Type (items 143/153) — **every existing core DocPerm row (all roles, all permlevels) replicated as a Custom DocPerm row first**, since adding a Custom DocPerm for just one role silently suppresses every core DocPerm role on that doctype ([[feedback_custom_docperm_suppresses_core]]); only Sales User's own permlevel-0 `create` flag was then flipped to 1. Deliberately **create-only, no write/delete** for Sales User — matches the established IB Transport/IB Branding "add but don't edit" convention for masters, not the fuller write access Market Segment got. Verified live via `has_permission` for a real Sales User (create=True, write=False on all 3) and confirmed Administrator/other roles' access unchanged.
    - Exported to `instabiz/fixtures/custom_docperm.json` via a new `hooks.py` fixtures filter block (`parent in [Territory, Campaign, Customer Group]`, no role filter — captures the full replicated set); `bench --site frontend clear-cache` run for the hooks.py change.
    - **Needs `bench restart`**: none — this item is permission/fixture/workspace-JSON only, live immediately (confirmed via `has_permission` against the live DB, not just the fixture file).

160. **Barcode + label-generation engine — production-readiness audit (2026-09-08)** — user asked, mid-session, to "make sure barcoding and label generating engine are solid and ready for prod." Full trace of every barcode/label code path in the app (`overrides/item.py`, `overrides/stock_scan.py`, `ib-stock-scan` page, `IB Container Import` + its `IB Container Label` print format — the only 3 real surfaces, confirmed via grep), then live end-to-end testing via `bench execute` on disposable data (real item, real warehouse, real submit → real Stock Entry → real barcode/QR PDF render → `pdfinfo`/`pdftotext` verified page count and content correct → fully cancelled/reverted, real stock confirmed back at 0 after cleanup).
    - **Real bug, fixed — `python-barcode`/`pyqrcode`/`pypng` were never declared as dependencies.** `ib_container_import.py`'s `get_barcode_data_uri()`/`get_qr_data_uri()` (the actual label-image generators, called from the print format's Jinja template) import `barcode` and `pyqrcode` — both happened to already be installed in this dev venv, but neither was listed in `pyproject.toml`'s `dependencies`. Same exact class of gap item 141 already found and fixed once for `pytesseract`/`pdf2image` — a fresh production deploy (`bench setup requirements` on a clean venv) would install cleanly, then crash with `ModuleNotFoundError` the first time anyone tried to print a label. Fixed by adding all 3 (`pypng` is `pyqrcode`'s PNG backend — needed for `png_as_base64_str`, not auto-pulled as a transitive dependency).
    - **Real bug, fixed — no upper bound on the manual "how many labels?" print-count prompt.** The Draft-container "Print Label" row action (item 149) lets an operator type an arbitrary label count with zero validation on either side — a typo (an extra zero) would try to synchronously render thousands of wkhtmltopdf pages in one web request inside `generate_item_labels()`, a real hang/crash risk for something reachable from the list view by any Stock role user. Added a `_MAX_LABEL_COUNT = 2000` cap, enforced server-side (`frappe.throw` beyond it, verified live for 0/-5/2001/500000 all correctly rejected, 5 correctly accepted) plus a client-side description note. **Deliberately NOT applied to `no_of_boxes` at submit** — that's a real recorded shipment quantity, not a free-typed guess, and hard-blocking a genuinely large stock receipt just because printing that many labels would be slow would be a worse outcome (a blocked real stock posting) than the slow render it's meant to prevent; considered and reverted after an initial pass added it there too.
    - **Real bug, fixed — `resolve_barcode()` (Scan Stock page) had no defense against an orphaned `Item Barcode` row or a disabled item.** If the parent `Item` no longer existed (a raw delete that skipped the normal cascade) the code did `item.item_name` on a `None` result → an uncaught `AttributeError` (ugly 500, not a real error message). Fixed with an explicit existence check + friendly `frappe.throw`. Also: a disabled Item's barcode could still be scanned in/out with no warning at all (the item search endpoint in `overrides/item.py` already excludes disabled items from lookup, but Scan Stock never checked) — fixed with the same friendly-throw pattern. `adjust_stock()` doesn't need the same guard — it goes through a real Stock Entry insert, which already throws its own clear "Item is disabled" error via core validation.
    - **Real bug, fixed — qty silently carried over between different scanned items on the Scan Stock page.** Scanning item A, typing a qty, then scanning item B without submitting left the old qty value sitting in the Qty field — a real risk of receiving/issuing the wrong quantity against the new item by mistake. Fixed: qty resets to 1 on every fresh successful resolve. Also fixed a smaller UX gap in the same pass — an unknown-barcode scan left the bad value sitting in the input with no re-focus, now clears and refocuses automatically.
    - **Biggest real gap, not a code bug — flagged to the user, not silently fixed**: barcode *generation* only ever happens as a side effect of passing an item through `IB Container Import` (`_resolve_barcode()`, sets `barcode = item_code`, Code128). There is no standalone "generate a barcode for this item" button or bulk tool anywhere in the app (confirmed via grep — same finding item 142 already made on 2026-08-25). Live count as of this audit: **4 of 536 enabled Items have any barcode at all** (up from item 142's "2 of 534," organic drift from the 2 real Container Imports run since). This means the Scan Stock page (`ib-stock-scan`) — code-correct and now hardened per the fixes above — is functionally near-useless today: scanning almost any real physical item in the warehouse returns "No item found for barcode," not because the feature is broken, but because the barcode simply doesn't exist yet for that item. **Not bulk-backfilled this session** — writing a barcode to ~530 real Item records company-wide is a real production-data decision (not reversible without deleting the same number of rows again), so it needs an explicit go-ahead rather than being done as part of an audit. If approved, the fix is mechanical: run `_resolve_barcode()`'s same item_code-as-barcode scheme across every enabled Item that doesn't have one yet.
    - Verified all fixes together end-to-end, not just individually: `py_compile`/`node --check` clean on every touched file; live disposable-data test (real item `IS-51210V-029TRNANL`, real warehouse `Second Floor - GUJARAT - IB`) — Container Import inserted → barcode auto-resolved to item_code → submitted → real Stock Entry posted 30 KG → label PDF rendered (3 pages, matching 3 boxes, `pdftotext` confirmed "ROLL NO" ×3, both "1 / 3" and "3 / 3" present, barcode+QR data-URIs embedded) → cancelled → deleted → confirmed real Bin qty back to 0. Scan Stock's `resolve_barcode()` tested live as a real Stock User against that same barcode (correct item+balance returned) and against an unknown barcode (correctly rejected) — no real disabled Item existed in this dataset to test that specific branch live, code-reviewed instead.
    - **Needs `bench restart`**: `stock_scan.py`, `ib_container_import.py` — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]; both already verified working live via `bench execute` (which reloads modules fresh each invocation) and via the running web process's autoreload, but a full restart hasn't been done to guarantee worker/scheduler processes see them too. The `pyproject.toml` dependency declarations need no restart — packages are already installed in this venv; the fix only matters for a *future* fresh-environment `bench setup requirements` run.

161. **Batch → Production → Serial traceability + IB Design System + full bug hunt (2026-09-10)** — spec at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-10-batch-serial-traceability-design.md`. Commits `1c9272f` (first pass, native Batch/Serial — **superseded**) then `5e2fb85` (final, lightweight annotation doctypes).
    - **Barcode engine** (from item 160's flagged gap): new `item_list.js` **"Generate Missing Barcodes"** button (Stock Manager/System Manager) — `instabiz.overrides.item.backfill_missing_barcodes(dry_run)`, Code128 = item_code, skips `_INTERNAL_ITEM_GROUPS` (`PACKAGING`) + non-stock + disabled. Ran it: **525 SKUs backfilled**. New read-only `Item.custom_barcode` (synced from the first Barcodes row by `sync_barcode_field` before_save hook) + rendered Code128 image on the Item **Details** tab (`item.js`). `python-barcode`/`pyqrcode`/`pypng` declared in `pyproject.toml`.
    - **Genealogy model** — end state after a mid-session rewrite: **do NOT use native ERPNext `Batch`/`Serial No`.** First pass enabled `has_batch_no`/`has_serial_no` on the catalogue (530 + 439 items) — this makes ERPNext **mandate batch/serial selection on every outward stock movement**, and **399 of 401 items with stock could not ship** (their existing stock is batch-null, FIFO auto-pick has nothing to select → `DN` submit throws "Serial No / Batch No are mandatory"). Also broke Container Import receipts ("Please set Serial No Series in the item"). **Both flags reverted**; genealogy is annotation-only via two new lightweight doctypes:
      - **`IB Batch`** (`autoname: field:batch_id`; fields: `batch_id`, `kind` RM/FG, `item`, `qty`, `status`, `source_type`, `container_import`, `purchase_receipt`, `supplier`, `supplier_lot`, `received_date`, `gsm`, `width_mm`, `work_order`, `parent_batches` JSON). **id = `{IB-CTN-####}::{item_code}::{row.idx}`** — uses `doc.name` NOT `container_no` (container_no is human-entered, not unique — two containers with the same no. silently shared/mislinked a batch; found by running the bug hunt twice).
      - **`IB FG Serial`** (`autoname: field:serial_no`; id = `{item_code}::{YYMMDD}::{seq04}`; genealogy fields + `box_no`, dimensions, `delivery_note`/`customer` for later dispatch stamping).
    - **Phase 1 (batch genealogy, annotation only, no stock-ledger change):**
      - `IB Container Import.on_submit` → `_make_batch()` per row creates an `IB Batch` (kind=Raw Material); the Material Receipt Stock Entry no longer sets `batch_no`. `on_cancel` deletes the IB Batches. Container-label QR trace-id uses `doc.name`.
      - `Purchase Receipt.on_submit` → `_make_grn_batches()` creates one `IB Batch` (kind=Raw Material, `purchase_receipt` link) per received line — **domestic purchase is the other genealogy root beside imports**. `on_cancel` deletes them.
      - `IB Work Order.source_batch` (Custom Field, Link → **IB Batch** — the `options` flip from "Batch" needs a **committing** script, not `bench execute frappe.db.set_value` which never commits; a stale "Batch" options value throws `LinkValidationError` on every `apply_workflow` WO start). `start_item_stage(order_sheet_item, stage, source_batch=None)` sets it; `_thread_source_batch()` carries it forward to every later-stage WO of the same order sheet item automatically. New whitelisted `set_wo_source_batch(work_order, source_batch)` for manual correction + `IB WO Batch Consumption` child table for multi-input.
      - New **`ib-trace` page** (`instabiz.overrides.traceability.get_trace(trace_id)` whitelisted) — resolves an IB Batch / IB Work Order / IB FG Serial id (or SKU barcode → info) and renders the full forward+backward chain (source doc → RM batch → WO stages → FG batch → serials → downstream SOs). Sleek vertical genealogy timeline UI.
    - **Phase 2 (FG serials at production):** final production stage completion (`complete_work_order` / `advance_to_next_stage`, when `doc.stage == _get_stage_route(...)[-1]`, item group in `_SERIAL_ITEM_GROUPS`) → `_generate_fg_serials()` creates an FG `IB Batch` (kind=Finished Good, `parent_batches=[source_batch]`) + N `IB FG Serial` (N = `logs_to_make` or `pcs_to_make` or 1, capped 2000), each stamped with the full genealogy. Writes `fg_batch` + `produced_serials` back on the WO. Never raises (wrapped, `frappe.log_error`). New **`IB Serial Label`** print format (doctype IB Work Order, per-serial page: Code128 = serial, QR = trace deep-link URL). WO side-panel dropdown gains **"Print Serial Labels (N)"** when `produced_serials > 0`.
    - **`ib-stock-scan` rebuilt** (modern card UI, `ibss-*` classes): `resolve_barcode()` now resolves **Item / IB Batch / IB FG Serial** — batch + serial scans are **trace-only** (no add/deduct) with an "Open Traceability" CTA; Item scans keep the add/deduct flow. Container-label QR fixed (was encoding the bare SKU code — inert on a phone camera — now a `…/app/ib-trace?id=<batch>` URL).
    - **Label barcode vs QR convention** (user asked): keep BOTH — **barcode (Code128) = the id string** for warehouse handheld laser scanners → Scan Stock field; **QR = a trace deep-link URL** for a phone camera → opens the Traceability page. Different jobs, not redundant.
    - **IB Design System** (app-wide sleek-redesign, foundation only): `instabiz.bundle.css` gained a `.ib-ui-*` component set (`card`, `stat`/`stat-grid`, `search`, `pill` + variants, `kv`, `table`, `chip`, `skeleton`, `empty`, `chain`/`node` timeline, `feed`, `toolbar`) + design tokens (`--ib-space-*`, `--ib-r-*`, `--ib-rm/fg/ok/warn/danger/info-bg/fg`), all theme-aware via Frappe CSS vars + `color-mix`. New `public/js/ib_ui.js` (`window.ibUI`) — markup helpers (`card`, `kv`, `pill`, `stat`, `statGrid`, `table`, `skeleton`, `empty`, `chip`, `link`, `icon` + a shared feather SVG set), loaded globally via `app_include_js`. `ib-trace`/`ib-stock-scan` keep their own `ibt-*`/`ibss-*` classes as the reference implementation; **every other custom page is meant to adopt `ib-ui-*` page-by-page** — that rollout is not done, this is just the shared foundation.
    - **`item.set_batch_no_for_fg` reverted to a no-op** (was auto-enabling `has_batch_no` on new items → would reintroduce the delivery bug for any new SKU); `backfill_batch_tracking`/`backfill_serial_tracking` removed. `_SERIAL_ITEM_GROUPS` kept (drives FG-serial generation only, unrelated to native `has_serial_no`).
    - **Container Import valuation-rate blocker fixed**: `_make_stock_entry` sets `allow_zero_valuation_rate` when neither the row rate nor the item-master `valuation_rate` is set (was hard-blocking submit — recurrence of the item 149/113 pattern).
    - **Full end-to-end bug hunt** (user: "all modules... this is bug hunt"): swept ~26 pages live via `browser-harness` (every custom dashboard, all doc list views Q/SO/DN/SI/PO/PR/PE/IB Batch/IB FG Serial, several Script Reports, Traceability, Scan Stock, Item Pricing, KB, Follow-Ups) — **zero crashes / error modals / permission failures**. Verified full chains via `bench` + browser: **Sales** Q→SO→DN (FIFO batch auto-pick — `Stock Settings` already had `auto_create_serial_and_batch_bundle_for_outward=1` + `pick_serial_and_batch_based_on=FIFO`, so a batched item ships fine)→SI (GL balanced Sales/IGST/Debtors)→PE (outstanding→0); **Purchase** PO→PR (IB Batch created)→PI (GL balanced); **Imports** Container Import → IB Batch + stock; **Production** start stage w/ source_batch → threaded → complete → FG batch + 6 serials; **Traceability** full chain both directions.
    - **Workspace**: `Traceability` + `FG Serials` + `IB Batches` shortcuts added to Instabiz Stock; `Traceability` + `FG Serials` to Instabiz Production (via the safe `doc.save()` method).
    - **Needs `bench restart`** for all Python changes (`traceability.py` [new], `production.py`, `purchase_receipt.py`, `stock_scan.py`, `item.py`, `ib_container_import.py`, `hooks.py`) to reach worker/scheduler — not run this session, per [[feedback_no_kill_process]]; everything verified live via web-process autoreload + `bench execute`.
    - **Still open**: per-UOM Container Import Item fields (SQMT vs PCS vs KG need different intake fields + qty formulas — user to supply the exact math); the `ib-ui-*` adoption rollout across the remaining ~24 custom pages; Phase 3 (real Manufacture Stock Entries, ledger-true genealogy — spec'd, not built); one disposable hunt SO (`IB-SGM-SO-01718`, carries the persistent trace-demo Order Sheet — leave until the demo is torn down).

162. **Status-remap bug sweep, hardening, Collections Priority, Replenishment Suggestions (2026-09-18)** — branch `fix/review-bugs-2026-09` (commits b6027f3, 5ffa100, a23b951, 16863a7); tested on a local frappe_docker copy (Frappe/ERPNext/HRMS v15 + india_compliance), **not deployed**.
    - **Root cause of a whole bug class**: `IbStatusMixin` stores remapped labels (Quotation Open/Replied→`Pending`, Ordered→`Confirmed`, Lost→`Cancelled`; SO Completed/Closed→`Confirmed`; DN Completed→`Confirmed`), so any SQL/filter on the raw ERPNext name silently matches nothing. Fixed in Main Dashboard, Business Pulse, Customer Health, Analytics Hub (rep open quotes/orders), winback (stale-quote nudges never fired), SO list "Delivered" badge, **IB Lost Deal Analysis (never listed a lost quotation)**, Daily Sales Order Backlog, MRP open-SO demand, fulfillment SLA. New `overrides/ib_status.py` `stored_statuses(doctype, *raw)` builds lists from STATUS_MAP — use it for any new status query. `scripts/check_status_literals.py` (stdlib, in Gitea CI) fails the build on a raw remapped status without its stored label; it flags all of the above on the old tree. Quotation `Expired` is allow-listed (quotation_expiry writes it directly).
    - Other fixes: `_auto_reconcile` no longer FIFO-links an on-account advance (`custom_advance_for_so`) to old invoices (double-count); `auto_absent` falls back to `Company.default_holiday_list` like HRMS; 30-day overdue alert text no longer claims "ORDERS BLOCKED"; its dedup filters on indexed `document_name`.
    - `instabiz/tests/test_core_paths.py` — 11 tests (`bench --site <site> run-tests --app instabiz --module instabiz.tests.test_core_paths`; needs `allow_tests`).
    - `overrides/indexes.py` now runs on `after_migrate` (idempotent) — 18 indexes incl. sales-person on Q/SO/DN/Customer, SO location, IB Work Order order_sheet/order_sheet_item, IB Order Sheet sales_order, Payment Entry advance/party.
    - SO credit-limit + overdue blocks: still **off** unless site_config `ib_so_credit_checks` = 1. When on, Sales/System Manager can submit past a block by filling new `Sales Order.custom_credit_override_reason` (logged on timeline).
    - New daily `einvoice_age_alert.run_einvoice_age_alert` — bell to Accounts for submitted SIs without IRN from day 20 of the 30-day IRP window (no-op unless GST Settings `enable_e_invoice`).
    - New reports: **IB Collections Priority** (per-customer, AR Aging basis, priority = lakh × (1 + oldest age/30) × health weight, WhatsApp wa.me reminder link, Sales Users scoped to own customers) and **IB Replenishment Suggestions** (SO demand, recent-month weighted, projected Bin qty, reorder point = demand × lead + safety, "Create Material Request" drafts an unsaved MR). Shortcuts: Collections Priority on Instabiz + Finance; Replenishment on Stock + Procurement. KB entries 162–164.
    - **Deploy note**: pushing to `develop` auto-deploys to prod via `.gitea/workflows/deploy.yml` — review/merge deliberately.

163. **Instabiz Settings, messaging, overtime, leave, print formats, quick actions (2026-09-19)** — branch `feat/erp-upgrade-2026-09` (rebased onto `feature/wo-per-run`, which is what the dev server runs); tested on the local frappe_docker copy, **not deployed**.
    - **Instabiz Settings** (single doctype, tabs Sales / Finance / Print / Stock & Buying / HR / Messaging). Read through `overrides/ib_settings.py` (`get`, `get_int`, `get_float`, `get_check`); every caller passes its old constant as the default, so an unsaved page changes nothing. Wired: advance approver + backup (also exposed to JS as `frappe.boot.ib_settings`), credit-limit check (default on, only acts when a Customer has a Credit Limit) and overdue block (default off) — `ib_so_credit_checks` still forces both; default delivery days, dispatch SLA, dormant days, stale quote/lead/cooldown, PO follow-up, batch expiry, IRN warn day, receipt bank/cost center, IB Payroll leave credit, replenishment lead/review days, dormant reassignment (on, 25 per rep per day cap on moves **and** notices, most-inactive first), print branding/banks/terms, OT basis, leave days, WhatsApp mode/API fields, weekly MR toggle.

WhatsApp = free wa.me link now; API mode posts JSON to `wa_api_url`. PDF link = download_pdf with a Document Share Key expiring after `pdf_link_valid_days`. Sends are logged as timeline comments.

Tax invoice: HSN summary, location bank (no hard-coded "(Mumbai)"), ORIGINAL FOR RECIPIENT, draft/cancel watermark, buyer PO in its own box. Outstanding Statement uses a jinja method instead of `frappe.call` (failed outside an HTTP request). The location company address is used only when that Address is enabled — MH and Chennai company addresses are disabled on dev, and 1,228 Gujarat SOs carry the MH address.

New reports: IB Payment Register, IB Cash Forecast, IB Sales Forecast, IB Dormant Accounts, IB Statutory Returns (PF ECR #~# text, ESIC csv; wages back-computed from PF 12 % / ESIC 0.75 %; UAN = provident_fund_account, IP = health_insurance_no), IB Complaints Analysis, IB Gate Pass Register, IB Loan Register, IB Pending Approvals. **IB Campaign** (+ Recipient; build_list / send / mark_sent / refresh_results; email via sendmail, WhatsApp API or wa.me links per rep). `overrides/ageing.py` rebucket (ranges filter) on IB AR/AP/Stock Ageing; Business Report top_n / min_amount; messaging.dispatch_details (e-way bill, LR, public attachments) appended to messages. 171. **Factory vs warehouse, floor transfers, consignment profitability (2026-09-20)** — same branch. `overrides/manufacturing_site.py`: Warehouse.custom_manufacturing_enabled (after_migrate; seeded on GUJARAT - IB), inherited down the tree (`is_factory`); `so_needs_production` (SO set_warehouse, else location, else line warehouses — lines usually carry the Stock Settings default Stores - IB, so they come last); `production._WAREHOUSE_ONLY_LOCATIONS` is now `_WarehouseOnlyLocations()` (dynamic: a location with nothing ticked); on-submit auto order sheet skipped and manual create_order_sheet refused for non-factory orders — they go straight to Delivery Note. Caches live in frappe.flags (frappe.local has no __dict__). `branch_transfer.same_premises` → direct Material Transfer between floors / sub-warehouses of one location, transit only between branches; dialog/list button renamed Stock Transfer. IB Container Import Item: selling_rate (default selling price list), expected_value, expected_margin, margin_pct; header expected_sale_value / expected_profit / expected_margin_pct. **IB Consignment Profitability** (Container / Container Line views): processed = non-cancelled runs whose source_batch is the container's IB Batch (revenue = FG produced × item's average DN rate, estimated), sold as is = DN lines of the same item FIFO across containers from import date, left at landed cost. _make_batch tolerates Item without gsm/width_mm columns.

172. **Smarter logic + two AI helpers (2026-09-20)** — same branch. **Promise date** `overrides/promise_date.py` (`get_promise(doc|sales_order)`; per line: free Bin stock under the location tree (actual − reserved, a submitted SO adds back its own pending) → Goods In Transit to the location (`custom_final_warehouse`, per_transferred) → factory: `production_twin._simulate_item` finish + 1 → else replenishment_lead_days; settings promise_dispatch_days / promise_transit_days in IB Sales Settings); headline + Details + "Use this date" on the SO form (`ib_sales_common.js`). **IB Slitting Plan** + `overrides/slitting_plan.py` (open Order Sheet lines whose route has Slitting, grouped by IB Production Recipe raw item, every Active RM IB Batch with width tried, best-fit-decreasing into passes ≤ width − slitting_trim_mm (20) and ≤ max Slitting knife_positions, least trim wins, tie = oldest; `create_pass` → `production_run.create_run` with source_qty = min(need, batch qty)). **Machine cost**: IB Machine.cost_per_hour (+ Stock Settings default_machine_cost_per_hour); `production_stock._add_conversion_cost` adds stage hours × rate as Stock Entry additional_costs (Expenses Included In Valuation) on the run's Repack; **IB Roll Cost** report (material / machine / unit cost vs 180-day selling rate, billing-mode basis). **Wastage**: `overrides/wastage_watch.check_run` on IB Work Order on_update → bell to Factory Management per stage event above machine wastage_norm_pct (else wastage_norm_default_pct 3), dedup marker `[ib-wastage-<event>]`; plus a timeline comment. IB Work Order has a Table field named `route`, which Frappe's Comment `update_comment_in_doc` read as a web route (redis DataError — every comment on a run failed); fixed by `overrides/comment.IBComment` (override_doctype_class Comment) skipping the route cache clear for `_TABLE_ROUTE_DOCTYPES`; **IB Wastage Analysis** report. **Order reader**: IB Document Intake `rule_extract` (no Claude: qty-with-unit lines, @ rate, party by first line / GSTIN), `spec_match_item` (width mm / length m / micron / colour + customer's past items), `match_customer_gstin`; `quick_read` behind SO list "Read Order". **Morning briefing** `overrides/briefing.py` cron 0 9 * * * (IB Messaging Settings morning_briefing / briefing_roles / briefing_min_overdue / briefing_use_ai; Preview / Send buttons on the settings form). **ICMS parity** on IB Container Import: freight_usd, insurance_usd, duty_rate_pct, bank_margin, cif_inr, bank_charge; landed = CIF × fx + duty + CIF × bank margin + CHA + other (same as ICMS costing.js); `before_insert` pre-fills ICMS defaults from IB Stock Settings import_default_*. `anthropic` added to pyproject (llm.py imports it).

173. **Tested on a copy of dev; rolls in m²; GST on invoice only; sidebar (2026-09-20)** — same branch. Local site = restored dev backup (see memory frappe-docker-local). Findings on real data and fixes: (1) orders are rarely closed with a Delivery Note (29 DNs vs 2,321 "Pending" SOs since March) → promise date / briefing use open orders of the last `promise_open_order_days` (30, IB Sales Settings) instead of Bin reserved qty, matched by the order's Location (dev line warehouses are MAHARASHTRA - IB even on Gujarat orders). (2) **Rolls**: tape items are stocked in SQMT but sold in PCS with conversion factor 1 → `utils.roll_area` / `apply_roll_area` (called from recalculate_items on Q/SO/DN/SI) sets conversion factor = width/1000 × length for ROLL_UOMS lines of SQMT items; patch `roll_area_on_open_orders` corrected 3,278 open SO lines and recomputed reserved qty; production `_finish_run` splits final m² by each output's planned m² and converts to rolls; Repack rows carry the m²-per-roll factor; slitting plan compares jumbo m² with order m² (`_m2`). (3) **GST**: `quotation.drop_gst` on SO and DN (freight Actual rows kept, template "Transport Charges Only - IB"); Sales Invoice re-applies the in/out-state template (place of supply for unregistered buyers) and calls calculate_taxes_and_totals — the template used to be set after totals, so 174 of 177 dev invoices had a template but no tax. (4) Slitting plan: raw material defaults to the item itself (item codes are jumbo specs; the SO line carries cut width/length), stock without an IB Batch counts as a jumbo (`STOCK::item::warehouse`, registered as an IB Batch on Create Run), lines of the same item are combined across orders; `create_pass(source_batch, order_sheet_items)` makes one run per order and splits the jumbo by width. (5) Sidebar: `workspace_merge.show_tabs` (after_migrate) shows Selling (was under hidden Instabiz CRM), CRM, Accounting (+ Payables / Receivables / Financial Reports) and builds a **Reports** workspace (all Instabiz reports + key ERPNext / HRMS / GST ones by area); fixed sidebar order. (6) IB Complaints Analysis / IB Tally Export default their filters. Comments on IB Work Order: `comment.IBComment` (see 172).

174. **Dimensions on stock / purchase / container rows; duplicate workspace entries (2026-09-20)** — same branch. `overrides/stock_dims.py` (after_migrate): Stock Entry Detail, Material Request Item, Stock Reconciliation Item get custom_thickness / color / width_mm / length_mtr / qty_pkg / total_pkg (fetch from Item when empty); Purchase Order / Receipt / Invoice Item get custom_thickness and their existing colour / width / length go into the grid. Frappe grids stop at 10 columns (grid.js total_colsize > 11), so each grid is laid out there (property setters: warehouses / qty / rate at 1 column, schedule date / stock uom / item tax template out of the grid). Stock Entry before_validate: rows in rolls of an SQMT item → conversion factor = width × length (not Repack / Manufacture). IB Container Import Item: color + custom_thickness, width / length fetched from Item, grid trimmed (item, dims, boxes, total qty, rate, landed rate) — before, the grid overflowed after width so length never showed; container and production stock entries carry the row dimensions (production outputs carry the cut size). Workspaces: later shortcuts had re-added Warehouse (Stock) and Document Intake (Selling / Buying) under new labels — removed; `workspace_merge.dedupe_workspace` runs on every visible workspace after migrate (also fixes HRMS Expense Claims listing Vehicle Expenses twice); Reports keeps the IB prefix when a label would clash (Stock Ageing).

175. **Row print everywhere, GST-inclusive receivables, e-way bill from invoice, own-data scoping, order reconciliation (2026-09-20)** — same branch. `public/js/ib_list_row_print.js` (app_include_js): Print (preview) button on every list row whose doctype can print (default print format), unless the list sets its own row button. **GST**: Sales Order / Delivery Note stay GST-free (internal) but Sales Order gets `custom_gst_amount` + `custom_total_with_gst` (`overrides/so_gst.py`, after_migrate fields; worked out on a copy with the invoice template; patch `so_gst_totals`). `billing_mode.sales_total_expr(alias)` (dev: COALESCE(NULLIF(custom_total_with_gst,0), grand_total)) is used for outstanding (`sales_outstanding_expr`), AR, collections, dashboards, statement, portal, briefing, cash forecast, incentive unpaid; the order PDF (`print_helpers.totals`) shows a "GST (charged on invoice)" line and the GST-inclusive total. OSPF_V2 (DB-only format) still prints grand_total. **e-Way Bill** only from Sales Invoice: DN on_submit hook removed, `ewaybill.after_migrate` turns GST Settings enable_e_waybill_from_dn off. No transport charges on invoice/DN (user). **Security**: portal `give_portal_login` refuses an email already tied to another customer; `_ocr_file` checks File read permission; dpr_auto prompt/stash need write on the run; campaign refresh_results and list_print outstanding letter check permission. **Own data** (`overrides/own_data.py`): `locked_user()` = Sales User without System/Sales Manager or Accounts User/Manager; permission hooks on 13 Instabiz doctypes (tickets, gate pass, campaign, customer score, sales target, sample request, document intake, credit note, customer assignment, PDC, price suggestion, customer item spec, customer share); reports lock inside execute (sales_person_user on Collections Report / Dispatch / Gross Margin / Lost Deal / Sales KPIs / SKU, own customers on Business Report, permission on Party Ledger, own tickets on Complaints, own customers on Price Suggestions); main dashboard shows own figures; Daily Sales + Territory reports managers only (after_migrate). **Reconciliation** (`overrides/production_reco.py`, report **IB Order Reconciliation**): per SO line ordered / produced (completed runs, IB WO Output.sales_order_item) / in run / dispatched; flags Dispatched more than produced, Dispatched without production, Over-dispatched, Produced short/over, Ready not dispatched (IB Stock Settings reco_tolerance_pct 5, reco_ready_days 3, reco_notify); on DN submit, run completion and daily; comment on the SO + bell to Factory Management and the rep once per line+flag.

176. **Print engine — one macro set, three layouts, 54 formats, letterhead (2026-09-20)** — same branch. `templates/print/ib_print_macros.html` + `overrides/print_helpers.py` (`ib_print(doc, kind, layout)` and the jinja helpers `ib_items`, `ib_totals`, `ib_hsn_summary`, `ib_statement`, `ib_stock_lines`, `ib_request_lines`, `ib_journal_lines`, `ib_gate_lines`, `ib_payment_refs`, `ib_note_totals`, `ib_money`, `ib_date`) drive 18 document templates under `templates/print/docs/` — quotation, sales order, proforma, delivery challan, tax invoice, purchase order, GRN, purchase invoice, payment, journal voucher, credit/debit note, expense voucher, PDC receipt, statement, stock entry, material request, gate pass. Each is published in three layouts (**Classic / Modern / Compact**) = 54 print formats under `instabiz/instabiz/print_format/<scrub>___<layout>/`, each a two-line include that sets `ib_layout`; `overrides/print_families.py` writes the folders, sets each doctype’s `default_print_format` via property setter and creates the default HTML Letter Head "Instabiz" on after_migrate. Tax invoice: Rule 48 copies (Original / Duplicate / Triplicate) in one PDF, HSN summary, e-invoice IRN + signed QR from `e-Invoice Log`, UPI QR (pyqrcode) when a UPI id is set. `pdf_footer` macro gives every format a page x of y footer. **Gotchas**: a print format folder must be named exactly `frappe.scrub(name)` ("Tax Invoice - Classic" → `tax_invoice___classic`); the Jinja print sandbox has no `frappe.conf` and no `frappe.utils.get_fullname`; Frappe’s own print CSS overrides table header colours unless the macro sets them `!important`; a custom (Jinja) format never renders Frappe’s letterhead, so the letterhead is drawn by the macro itself. Location (address / GSTIN / bank) is taken from the document’s own `custom_location`, else derived from its warehouses — a Gujarat stock transfer used to print the Maharashtra address. Known gap: `OSPF_V2` (a DB-only Sales Order format on dev) still prints the total without GST; the full e-way bill page exists only in the legacy IB GST Tax Invoice format.

177. **Dashboard kit — theme, charts, filters, personalisation (2026-09-20)** — same branch. **`public/js/ib_dash_kit.js`** (`window.ibDash`, app_include_js after `ib_ui.js`): `chart(el, opts)` — theme-aware frappe.Chart wrapper (dark/light palette, short y-axis numbers, ₹ tooltips, empty state, destroys the previous chart so repeated refreshes stop stacking `<svg>`s, which is what produced the inner scrollbar and stray dots on the main dashboard); `filters(page, spec)` — filter bar in `page.page_form` with period presets (Today … This Year / Custom → from/to), Location, Sales Person, saved per user+page in localStorage, plus a "Reset filters" menu item; `personalise(page, spec)` — show / hide / reorder `[data-card]` cards from a "Customise layout…" menu item, saved per user, new cards appear for users who already have a layout; `range()`, `store()`/`read()`. It also patches `frappe.Chart` itself with `shortenYAxisNumbers` + `truncateLegends` defaults, so the pages built before the kit get readable axes without being rewritten. **`ib_ui.js`** gained `shortNum` / `moneyFull` / `moneyShort` (₹1.2 Cr · ₹4.5 L · ₹12.3 K), `deltaChip`, `sparkline`, `kpi` / `kpiGrid` and `chartCard`; `.ib-ui-kpi` keeps the value on one line (the old KPI card wrapped and left the ₹ on a line of its own). **Main dashboard** rebuilt on the kit: period / location / sales-person filters, 7 KPIs with deltas against the same-length previous window and a 6-month sparkline, trend chart with Value / Count tabs and day-week-month buckets picked from the range, location donut, top customers and sales-person rankings, recent orders, quick actions — all as reorderable cards; `get_dashboard_data(from_date, to_date, location, sales_person)`. **HR dashboard** rebuilt (it was "basically useless"): new `get_hr_overview(month, department, branch)` — headcount, joiners/leavers, 12-month attrition, in/out today, attendance rate against last month, late arrivals, pending approvals (leave + OT + F&F), payroll, overtime hours, day-by-day attendance, attendance mix, headcount by department, 6-month salary cost, leave by type, most-absent list and birthdays / work anniversaries in the next 30 days; the attendance / leave / payroll lists stay as they were, below the analytics. **Finance** and **Procurement** got the same filter bar (period + location) end to end (`get_finance_data` / `get_procurement_data` take `from_date`, `to_date`, `location`; balances, aging and open POs stay "as of now"), card personalisation and kit charts; their nav buttons moved into the page menu. **Collections** got a Location filter. **Business Pulse** chart goes through the kit. CSS: `.ib-ui-kpi*`, `.ib-ui-delta`, `.ib-ui-spark`, `.ib-ui-chart`, `.ib-dash-cards` 12-column grid, `.ib-dash-rank`, `.ib-pz*` (personalisation dialog), and one block that pulls the four pre-kit KPI card styles (`.ib-fin-kpi`, `.ib-proc-kpi`, `.ib-col-kpi`, `.ib-ch-kpi`) onto the same look — prefixed `body ` because those pages inject their own `<style>` into `<head>` at runtime and would otherwise win on source order. **Real bug fixed on the way**: `.ib-ui-bar .fill` is a `<span>` with `width:%` — inline elements ignore width, so every design-system progress bar had been rendering empty since it was written; both `.track` and `.fill` are now `display:block`. Tested against the restored dev copy: main dashboard ₹9.2 Cr / 285 orders for September, Gujarat 6.39 Cr matching the finance dashboard’s own Gujarat figure, HR 54 active people / 29.5% attendance rate / 18.5% attrition. **Not done**: Customer Health keeps its territory filter (Customer has no location field of its own); Analytics Hub already had its own filters and chart handling and was left alone beyond the global chart defaults.

178. **Dashboards tab — ours and ERPNext’s merged into one list, native charts repaired (2026-09-20)** — same branch. `overrides/dashboards.py`. Found first: the site has **14 native `Dashboard` records** (Selling, CRM, Accounts, Buying, Stock, Manufacturing, Human Resource, Payroll, Attendance, Recruitment, Employee Lifecycle, Expense Claims, Project, Asset), 73 Dashboard Charts and 59 Number Cards, and **not one `Workspace Link` pointed at any of them** — they existed but had no nav path. New public workspace **Dashboards** (first in the sidebar, rebuilt by `build_dashboards_workspace()` from `show_tabs()` on after_migrate): 24 links in five cards (Overview / Sales / Finance / Operations / People) plus shortcut tiles for Dashboard, Business Pulse and Analytics Hub. **No duplicates** (user’s call): `IB_EQUIVALENT` maps the nine native dashboards an Instabiz page already covers (Selling→Dashboard, CRM→Customer Board, Accounts→Finance, Buying→Procurement, Stock→Live Stock Balance, Manufacturing→Production, Human Resource / Payroll / Attendance→HR Dashboard) and those are left out; the remaining five are only listed when their module actually has rows (`_has_data`), which today it does not — Project / Asset / Job Applicant / Job Opening / Expense Claim / Employee Onboarding / Promotion / Separation / Timesheet / Quality Inspection / native Work Order / Opportunity are all **0 records** — so the native card is empty and appears on its own the day someone uses one of those modules. **Chart health**: `check_dashboards()` (static: does the chart’s doctype / report exist), `smoke_dashboards()` (renders all 73 charts and 59 cards for real) and `dashboard_health()` (whitelisted, System Manager). Two Frappe core quirks to know: `dashboard_chart.get(chart_name=..., no_cache=1)` throws — `cache_source` hands `get()` a Document which then calls `parse_json` on it, so use `refresh=1`; and `get()` is only for Count / Sum / Average / Group By charts — Report charts go through the report runner and Custom charts through their source method, which is what `_run_chart()` dispatches. **Five standard charts were genuinely broken** (they throw in the UI too), each missing a filter the report insists on: Profit and Loss and Budget Variance (`from_fiscal_year` / `to_fiscal_year`), Attendance Count (`month` / `year`), Oldest Items and Item-wise Annual Sales (`to_date`) — `repair_standard_charts()` fills them with the current period and runs on after_migrate, since a migrate re-imports the standard records. Charts failing went 29 → 1. **The last one is a data finding, not a code bug**: "Item-wise Annual Sales" still throws because **48 Sales Order lines (31 Mar – 3 Apr 2026, the original import window) reference item codes that no longer exist in `tabItem`** (IS-53133VE-120BLRBNL, IS-52143V-130CRRBNL, IS-311-039GB …) and ERPNext’s own report does `item_details.get(code).get("item_name")` on the missing row; Delivery Note and Sales Invoice lines are clean (0 orphans). Left for the user to decide — recreate the items or correct the orders. **Tests**: `instabiz/tests/test_core_paths.py` gains `TestDashboards` — every dashboard RPC answers, the three filtered ones accept `from_date` / `to_date` / `location` end to end (this is the check that catches a page calling a signature the backend does not have), every Dashboards-workspace link target exists, no native dashboard is listed next to its Instabiz equivalent, and every number card computes. 20 tests pass.
